How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Powers
Aliases: Accessory Bag Powers, Accessory Powers, Fortuitous
Powers are a mechanic that apply stat bonuses to [[Accessory Bag|Accessory Bags]]. They replace the [[Reforging]] on [[Accessories]].
## Mechanics
The player is able to apply Powers to their Accessories by interacting with [[Maxwell]]. Each Power gives different stat bonuses, which scale based on the player's [[Accessory Power]].
## List of Powers
### Default
Normal Powers do not require [[Power Stones]] to be unlocked, but some require [[Combat|Combat XV (15)]] to be unlocked.
calcpage  = Calculator:Magical power
 template  = Template:Calculator/Magical_power
 calcname  = Magical Power Calculator
 form      = magicalPowerCalcForm
 result    = inner

 param     = uncommon|Uncommons|0|int|0-999999
 param     = rare|Rares|0|int|0-999999
 param     = epic|Epics|0|int|0-999999
 param     = legendary|Legendaries|0|int|0-999999
 param     = mythic|Mythics|0|int|0-999999
 param     = special|Specials|0|int|0-999999
 param     = very_special|Very Specials|0|int|0-999999

### Stone Powers
[[Power Stones]] can be combined to unlock new Stone Powers. Stone Powers also have Unique Power Bonuses, depending on the Power. Each has a different required [[Combat]] level

 template  = :Power Stones/List of Power Stones
 form      = PowerStonesStatBonusesCalcForm
 result    = PowerStonesStatBonusesCalcResult

 param     = magical_power|Accessory Power|250|int

## Trivia
- In the description of a [[Combat]] level with unlockable Powers, the Powers are still referred to as [[Reforging|Reforges]].
- There are, as of SkyBlock 0.27.2, 151 Accessories in the game (excluding their own upgrades) and the maximum Accessory Power is [[Accessory Power|2,184 Accessory Power]] and another [[Accessory Power|252 Accessory Power]] from Dungeon Accessories when in dungeons, adding up to [[Accessory Power|2,436 Accessory Power]] in [[Dungeons]]. On top of that, 68 Accessories can be [[Enrichments|Enriched]].

# Rare Crops
Aliases: Rare Crop
[Rare Crops]
Rare Crops are items that can be obtained rarely from [[Farming]]. The chance to drop Rare Crops can be increased with [[Overbloom]].
## List of Rare Crops
Rare Crops include the drops from in-season crops during the [[Harvest Feast]], drops from [[Tater Armor]] and its upgrades, and an assortment of other items.

# Reclaiming
Aliases: Reclaim
Reclaiming is a system where outdated upgrades used on items can be refunded or fixed. The Reclaiming system can be accessed with the /reclaim command.
If an item can be Reclaimed, this message will appear in its description: This item can be reclaimed! Use /reclaim to take back what's rightfully yours!
## List of Reclaims
|Name|Available Reclaims|Reclaimable Items|
|-|-|-|

## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 2, 2025|0.23.4|Added Reclaiming.|
|Sep 2, 2025|0.23.4|Wood Singularities ([[Wood Singularity]]) used on Wooden Swords can now be Reclaimed.|
|Sep 2, 2025|0.23.4|Dungeonized ([[Dungeons]]) Items that are no longer dungeonizable can now be Reclaimed.|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Certain Upgrades added to [[Axes]] which have been recategorized as [[Farming Tools]] can now be Reclaimed.|
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Old [[Boosters]] applied to [[Foraging]] Gear can now be Reclaimed into Common Boosters ([[Boosters]]).|
